class="ToolCodeBlock-module__code__X8Whj"><code class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeWrap__HyFh6">$ sed -n <span class="hljs-string">'1,120p'</span> /home/runner/work/nn.nn/nn.nn/lang/a9nn/NNECCOAgent.lua
-- a9nn/NNECCOAgent.lua
-- Neural Network Embodied Cognitive Coprocessor Orchestrator agent.
--
-- Integrates:
--   • Echo State Reservoir Network <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> temporal processing
--   • Multi-layer consciousness pipeline (L0-L3)
--   • Emotion Processing Unit
--   • OpenCog AtomSpace knowledge graph
--   • Personality system
--   • Parallel LLaMA.cpp orchestration (1-9 instances)
--   • EchoBeats 12-step cognitive loop
--   • Hardware-style register interface

<span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> ns     = require(<span class="hljs-string">'a9nn.nn_ns'</span>)
<span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> Tensor = ns.Tensor
<span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> Class  = ns.Class
<span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> nn     = ns   -- <span class="hljs-built_in">alias</span>; TruthValue resolved lazily below

<span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> NNECCOAgent, parent = Class.class(<span class="hljs-string">'nn.NNECCOAgent'</span>, <span class="hljs-string">'nn.CognitiveAgent'</span>)

-- ─── EchoBeats step names ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

<span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> ECHO_BEATS = {
   <span class="hljs-string">"PERCEIVE"</span>,    -- 1  raw sensory input
   <span class="hljs-string">"FILTER"</span>,      -- 2  attentional filtering
   <span class="hljs-string">"RESONATE"</span>,    -- 3  reservoir <span class="hljs-built_in">echo</span>
   <span class="hljs-string">"ENCODE"</span>,      -- 4  semantic encoding
   <span class="hljs-string">"RECALL"</span>,      -- 5  episodic memory lookup
   <span class="hljs-string">"REASON"</span>,      -- 6  LLaMA inference
   <span class="hljs-string">"EMOTE"</span>,       -- 7  emotion update
   <span class="hljs-string">"PLAN"</span>,        -- 8  action selection
   <span class="hljs-string">"LEARN"</span>,       -- 9  RL update
   <span class="hljs-string">"REFLECT"</span>,     -- 10 meta-cognition
   <span class="hljs-string">"EXPRESS"</span>,     -- 11 output generation
   <span class="hljs-string">"INTEGRATE"</span>,   -- 12 state integration
}

-- ─── Consciousness layers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

<span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> CONSCIOUSNESS_LAYERS = {
   [0] = <span class="hljs-string">"DORMANT"</span>,
   [1] = <span class="hljs-string">"REACTIVE"</span>,
   [2] = <span class="hljs-string">"DELIBERATE"</span>,
   [3] = <span class="hljs-string">"META"</span>,
}

-- ─── Emotion channels ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

<span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> EMOTIONS = {
   <span class="hljs-string">"curiosity"</span>, <span class="hljs-string">"joy"</span>, <span class="hljs-string">"surprise"</span>, <span class="hljs-string">"fear"</span>,
   <span class="hljs-string">"disgust"</span>,   <span class="hljs-string">"anger"</span>, <span class="hljs-string">"sadness"</span>, <span class="hljs-string">"anticipation"</span>,
}

-- ─── Constructor ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

--- @param opts  table:
--   stateSize          input state dimensionality (default 64)
--   actionSize         number of actions (default 8)
--   reservoirSize      ESN neuron count (default 847)
--   llamaInstances     number of LLaMA.cpp instances (default 1)
--   basePort           first LLaMA server port (default 8080)
--   personalityArchetype  <span class="hljs-string">"NeuroSama"</span> | <span class="hljs-string">"Analytical"</span> | nil (default <span class="hljs-string">"NeuroSama"</span>)
--   consciousnessLayer    initial layer 0-3 (default 1)
--   verbose            <span class="hljs-built_in">enable</span> diagnostic output (default <span class="hljs-literal">false</span>)
<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> NNECCOAgent:__init(opts)
   opts = opts or {}
   -- Set defaults before calling parent
   opts.stateSize  = opts.stateSize  or 64
   opts.actionSize = opts.actionSize or 8

   parent.__init(self, opts)

   -- ── Reservoir ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> EchoReservoir = Class.get(<span class="hljs-string">'nn.EchoReservoir'</span>)
   self.reservoir = EchoReservoir(
      opts.stateSize,
      opts.reservoirSize or 847,
      {
         spectralRadius = opts.spectralRadius or 0.9,
         leakRate       = opts.leakRate       or 0.7,
         sparsity       = opts.sparsity       or 0.85,
      }
   )

   -- ── Consciousness layer ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   self.consciousnessLayer = opts.consciousnessLayer or 1
   self.consciousnessHistory = {}

   -- ── Emotion processing ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   self.emotions = Tensor.zeros(#EMOTIONS)
   self.emotionNames = EMOTIONS
   -- Start with mild curiosity
   self.emotions.data[1] = 0.6

   -- ── Personality (override archetype) ──────────────────────────────────────
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> Personality = Class.get(<span class="hljs-string">'nn.Personality'</span>)
   <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> Personality <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span>
      <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> <span class="hljs-built_in">arch</span> = opts.personalityArchetype or <span class="hljs-string">"NeuroSama"</span>
      <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> <span class="hljs-built_in">arch</span> == <span class="hljs-string">"NeuroSama"</span> and Personality.NeuroSama <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span>
         self.personality = Personality.NeuroSama()
      elseif <span class="hljs-built_in">arch</span> == <span class="hljs-string">"Analytical"</span> and Personality.Analytical <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span>
         self.personality = Personality.Analytical()
      <span class="hljs-keyword">else</span>
         self.personality = Personality(nil, opts.personalityOpts)
      end
   end

   -- ── LLaMA orchestrator ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> LLaMAOrchestrator = Class.get(<span class="hljs-string">'nn.LLaMAOrchestrator'</span>)
   self.llama = LLaMAOrchestrator({
      numInstances = opts.llamaInstances or 1,
      basePort     = opts.basePort       or 8080,
      modelPath    = opts.modelPath      or <span class="hljs-string">""</span>,
      stubMode     = opts.stubMode,      -- nil = auto-detect
      verbose      = opts.verbose or <span class="hljs-literal">false</span>,
   })
   self.llama:initialize()

   -- ── Cognitive pipeline (post-reservoir encoder) ───────────────────────────
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> reservoirN = self.reservoir.reservoirN

8-12)</span></span></button></div><div><div class="Tool-module__toolContentMissionControl__fqP1_"><div class="markdown-body MarkdownRenderer-module__container__sJ8jf" data-copilot-markdown="true" style="--MarkdownRenderer_streaming-fade-duration: 750ms;"><figure class="ToolCodeBlock-module__container__EQXTk"><div class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeContainer__YJrZ9"><pre class="ToolCodeBlock-module__code__X8Whj"><code class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeWrap__HyFh6">$ sed -n <span class="hljs-string">'300,477p'</span> /home/runner/work/nn.nn/nn.nn/lang/a9nn/NNECCOAgent.lua
-- Surprise from large input change
   self.emotions.data[3] = math.min(1, math.max(0,
      math.abs(novelty) * 0.5))

   self.registers.R3 = self.emotions:<span class="hljs-built_in">clone</span>()
   ctx.log[#ctx.log+1] = string.format(<span class="hljs-string">"EMOTE: curiosity=%.2f joy=%.2f"</span>,
      self.emotions.data[1], self.emotions.data[2])
end

<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> NNECCOAgent:_beat_PLAN(ctx)
   self.beatMetrics[<span class="hljs-string">"PLAN"</span>] = self.beatMetrics[<span class="hljs-string">"PLAN"</span>] + 1
   -- Emotion-modulated action selection
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> emotCtx = Tensor.<span class="hljs-built_in">cat</span>(ctx.encoded, self.emotions)
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> modulated = self.emotionModulator:forward(emotCtx)

   -- Use the modulated context to produce Q-values via policy
   -- (policy takes stateSize input; we use original filtered input)
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> qvals = self.policy:forward(ctx.filteredInput)
   self.registers.R4 = qvals
   ctx.qvals = qvals

   -- Select action (with ε-greedy <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> training mode)
   <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> self.train <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span>
      ctx.action = self:act(ctx.filteredInput)
   <span class="hljs-keyword">else</span>
      <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> bestA, bestQ = 1, -math.huge
      <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> a = 1, self.actionSize <span class="hljs-keyword">do</span>
         <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> qvals.data[a] &gt; bestQ <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span> bestQ = qvals.data[a]; bestA = a end
      end
      ctx.action = bestA
   end

   ctx.log[#ctx.log+1] = string.format(<span class="hljs-string">"PLAN: action=%d qmax=%.4f"</span>,
      ctx.action, qvals:max())
end

<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> NNECCOAgent:_beat_LEARN(ctx)
   self.beatMetrics[<span class="hljs-string">"LEARN"</span>] = self.beatMetrics[<span class="hljs-string">"LEARN"</span>] + 1
   -- Store experience <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> we have a complete transition
   <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> ctx.prevState and ctx.action and ctx.reward ~= nil <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span>
      self:remember(ctx.prevState, ctx.action, ctx.reward,
                    ctx.filteredInput, ctx.done or <span class="hljs-literal">false</span>)
   end
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> loss = self:learn()
   ctx.loss   = loss
   ctx.log[#ctx.log+1] = loss
      and string.format(<span class="hljs-string">"LEARN: loss=%.6f eps=%.4f"</span>, loss, self.epsilon)
      or  <span class="hljs-string">"LEARN: memory too small, skipped"</span>
end

<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> NNECCOAgent:_beat_REFLECT(ctx)
   self.beatMetrics[<span class="hljs-string">"REFLECT"</span>] = self.beatMetrics[<span class="hljs-string">"REFLECT"</span>] + 1
   -- Meta-cognition: adjust consciousness layer
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> loss = ctx.loss or 0
   <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> loss &gt; 0.5 <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span>
      -- High error → increase deliberation
      self.consciousnessLayer = math.min(3, self.consciousnessLayer + 1)
   elseif loss &lt; 0.01 and loss &gt; 0 <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span>
      -- Low error → can reduce to reactive
      self.consciousnessLayer = math.max(1, self.consciousnessLayer - 1)
   end
   self.consciousnessHistory[#self.consciousnessHistory+1] = self.consciousnessLayer
   ctx.log[#ctx.log+1] = string.format(<span class="hljs-string">"REFLECT: layer=%d"</span>,
      self.consciousnessLayer)
end

<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> NNECCOAgent:_beat_EXPRESS(ctx)
   self.beatMetrics[<span class="hljs-string">"EXPRESS"</span>] = self.beatMetrics[<span class="hljs-string">"EXPRESS"</span>] + 1
   -- Primary output: use LLaMA response <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> available, <span class="hljs-keyword">else</span> describe action
   <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> ctx.llamaResponse and ctx.llamaResponse.text and ctx.llamaResponse.text ~= <span class="hljs-string">""</span> <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span>
      ctx.output = {
         text         = ctx.llamaResponse.text,
         action       = ctx.action,
         consciousness = CONSCIOUSNESS_LAYERS[self.consciousnessLayer],
      }
   <span class="hljs-keyword">else</span>
      ctx.output = {
         text   = string.format(<span class="hljs-string">"Action %d selected (Q=%.3f)"</span>,
            ctx.action, ctx.qvals and ctx.qvals.data[ctx.action] or 0),
         action = ctx.action,
         consciousness = CONSCIOUSNESS_LAYERS[self.consciousnessLayer],
      }
   end
   ctx.log[#ctx.log+1] = <span class="hljs-string">"EXPRESS: "</span> .. ctx.output.text:sub(1, 60)
end

<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> NNECCOAgent:_beat_INTEGRATE(ctx)
   self.beatMetrics[<span class="hljs-string">"INTEGRATE"</span>] = self.beatMetrics[<span class="hljs-string">"INTEGRATE"</span>] + 1
   -- Store current state <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> next cycle<span class="hljs-string">'s recall
   ctx.prevState = ctx.filteredInput

   -- Update knowledge base with current experience
   if self.knowledge then
      -- Resolve TruthValue from the AtomSpace module (already loaded)
      local atomMod = require('</span>a9nn.AtomSpace<span class="hljs-string">')
      local tv = atomMod.TruthValue.new(math.max(0, 1 - (ctx.loss or 0)), 1)
      -- Add an episode node for this cycle with a proper TruthValue object
      self.knowledge:addNode("EpisodeNode", "cycle_" .. self.cycleCount, tv)
   end

   -- Advance registers
   self.registers.PC = (self.registers.PC % 12) + 1
   ctx.log[#ctx.log+1] = string.format("INTEGRATE: cycle=%d complete", self.cycleCount)
end

-- ─── Utility ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

--- Encode a text string as a fixed-size feature vector.
-- Uses character n-gram frequencies mapped to (stateSize,) Tensor.
function NNECCOAgent:_encodeText(text)
   local n   = self.stateSize
   local out = Tensor.zeros(n)
   -- Simple char-level hashing into bins
   for i = 1, #text do
      local c   = string.byte(text, i)
      local bin = ((c * 31 + i * 7) % n) + 1
      out.data[bin] = out.data[bin] + 1
   end
   -- L2-normalise
   local norm = out:norm()
   if norm &gt; 1e-6 then
      for i = 1, n do out.data[i] = out.data[i] / norm end
   end
   return out
end

--- Return a hardware-style status dump.
function NNECCOAgent:getHardwareStatus()
   local llamaStatus = self.llama:getStatus()
   return {
      agentID     = self.id,
      cycle       = self.cycleCount,
      reservoir   = {
         size          = self.reservoir.reservoirN,
         spectralRadius = self.reservoir.spectralRadius,
         leakRate      = self.reservoir.leakRate,
         stateNorm     = self.registers.R1:norm(),
      },
      consciousness = {
         layer    = self.consciousnessLayer,
         name     = CONSCIOUSNESS_LAYERS[self.consciousnessLayer],
         history  = self.consciousnessHistory,
      },
      emotions = (function()
         local t = {}
         for i, name in ipairs(self.emotionNames) do
            t[name] = self.emotions.data[i]
         end
         return t
      end)(),
      llama       = llamaStatus,
      registers   = {
         PC  = self.registers.PC,
         STA = self.registers.STA,
      },
      beatMetrics = self.beatMetrics,
      memory      = {
         size     = self.memory:size(),
         capacity = self.memory.capacity,
      },
      policy = {
         epsilon = self.epsilon,
         steps   = self.stepCount,
      },
   }
end

function NNECCOAgent:__tostring__()
   return string.format(
      "nn.NNECCOAgent(id=%d, reservoir=%d, llama=%d, layer=%s)",
      self.id,
      self.reservoir.reservoirN,
      self.llama.numInstances,
      CONSCIOUSNESS_LAYERS[self.consciousnessLayer])
end

return NNECCOAgent

class="Tool-module__toolContentMissionControl__fqP1_"><div class="markdown-body MarkdownRenderer-module__container__sJ8jf" data-copilot-markdown="true" style="--MarkdownRenderer_streaming-fade-duration: 750ms;"><figure class="ToolCodeBlock-module__container__EQXTk"><div class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeContainer__YJrZ9"><pre class="ToolCodeBlock-module__code__X8Whj"><code class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeWrap__HyFh6">$ sed -n <span class="hljs-string">'1,120p'</span> /home/runner/work/nn.nn/nn.nn/lang/a9nn/EchoReservoir.lua
-- a9nn/EchoReservoir.lua
-- Echo State Network (ESN / Liquid State Machine) reservoir layer.
--
-- An ESN consists of:
--   • A fixed (untrained) recurrent reservoir of N neurons
--   • An input weight matrix W_in  (N × inSize)
--   • A reservoir weight matrix W_res (N × N)  with spectral radius &lt; 1
--   • An optional output layer (trained separately or via nn.Linear)
--
-- The reservoir maps a sequence of inputs to a high-dimensional state
-- trajectory, enabling rich temporal feature extraction.
--
-- Reference: Jaeger (2001) <span class="hljs-string">"The echo state approach to analysing and training
-- recurrent neural networks"</span>

<span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> nn     = require(<span class="hljs-string">'a9nn.nn_ns'</span>)
<span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> Tensor = nn.Tensor
<span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> Class  = nn.Class

<span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> EchoReservoir, parent = Class.class(<span class="hljs-string">'nn.EchoReservoir'</span>, <span class="hljs-string">'nn.Module'</span>)

--- Constructor.
-- @param inSize      dimensionality of each input step
-- @param reservoirN  number of reservoir neurons (default 100)
-- @param opts        table of optional parameters:
--   spectralRadius   target spectral radius  (default 0.9)
--   sparsity         fraction of zero weights (default 0.8)
--   leakRate         leaking rate α ∈ (0,1]  (default 1.0)
--   inputScaling     scaling <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> W_in         (default 1.0)
<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> EchoReservoir:__init(inSize, reservoirN, opts)
   parent.__init(self)
   opts = opts or {}

   self.inSize        = inSize
   self.reservoirN    = reservoirN or 100
   self.spectralRadius = opts.spectralRadius or 0.9
   self.sparsity       = opts.sparsity       or 0.8
   self.leakRate       = opts.leakRate       or 1.0
   self.inputScaling   = opts.inputScaling   or 1.0

   -- Initialise fixed weight matrices
   self:_initWeights()

   -- Current hidden state
   self.state = Tensor.zeros(self.reservoirN)

   -- Output = concatenation of state (and optionally input)
   self.output = Tensor.zeros(self.reservoirN)
end

<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> EchoReservoir:_initWeights()
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> N   = self.reservoirN
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> M   = self.inSize

   -- W_in: (N × M) scaled uniform
   self.W_in = Tensor.zeros(N, M)
   self.W_in:uniform(self.inputScaling)

   -- W_res: (N × N) sparse, rescaled to desired spectral radius
   self.W_res = Tensor.zeros(N, N)
   <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> i = 1, N <span class="hljs-keyword">do</span>
      <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> j = 1, N <span class="hljs-keyword">do</span>
         <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> math.random() &gt; self.sparsity <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span>
            self.W_res.data[(i-1)*N+j] = (math.random() * 2 - 1)
         end
      end
   end

   -- Approximate spectral radius by power iteration
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> rho = self:_powerIteration(self.W_res, 20)
   <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> rho &gt; 1e-6 <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span>
      <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> scale = self.spectralRadius / rho
      <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> i = 1, N*N <span class="hljs-keyword">do</span>
         self.W_res.data[i] = self.W_res.data[i] * scale
      end
   end
end

--- Approximate largest eigenvalue magnitude via power iteration.
<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> EchoReservoir:_powerIteration(W, iters)
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> N = W.size_[1]
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> v = Tensor.zeros(N)
   -- Random start
   <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> i = 1, N <span class="hljs-keyword">do</span> v.data[i] = math.random() * 2 - 1 end

   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> norm = v:norm()
   <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> norm &lt; 1e-10 <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span> <span class="hljs-built_in">return</span> 0 end
   <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> i = 1, N <span class="hljs-keyword">do</span> v.data[i] = v.data[i] / norm end

   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> rho = 0
   <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> _ = 1, iters <span class="hljs-keyword">do</span>
      <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> Wv = W:<span class="hljs-built_in">mv</span>(v)
      rho = Wv:norm()
      <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> rho &lt; 1e-10 <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span> <span class="hljs-built_in">break</span> end
      <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> i = 1, N <span class="hljs-keyword">do</span> v.data[i] = Wv.data[i] / rho end
   end
   <span class="hljs-built_in">return</span> rho
end

--- Reset the reservoir state to zeros.
<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> EchoReservoir:resetState()
   self.state:zero()
end

--- Forward pass <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> a SINGLE <span class="hljs-keyword">time</span> step.
-- @param input  Tensor of shape (inSize,)
-- @<span class="hljs-built_in">return</span>       Tensor of shape (reservoirN,) — reservoir state
<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> EchoReservoir:updateOutput(input)
   assert(input.size_[1] == self.inSize,
      string.format(<span class="hljs-string">"EchoReservoir: expected input size %d, got %d"</span>,
         self.inSize, input.size_[1]))

   -- pre-activation = W_in · u + W_res · x(t-1)
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> u  = self.W_in:<span class="hljs-built_in">mv</span>(input)
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> Wx = self.W_res:<span class="hljs-built_in">mv</span>(self.state)
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> pre = u:add(Wx)

   -- activation with tanh
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> newState = pre:apply(math.tanh)
&lt;shellId: 4 completed with <span class="hljs-built_in">exit</span> code 0&gt;
</code></pre></div></figure></div></div></div></div><div class="Tool-module__toolContainer__PQCpK Tool-module__toolContainerExpanded__tkI3R" style="--tool-preview-height-threshold: 475px;"><div class="Tool-module__toolContentMissionControl__fqP1_"><div class="markdown-body MarkdownRenderer-module__container__sJ8jf" data-copilot-markdown="true" style="--MarkdownRenderer_streaming-fade-duration: 750ms;"><figure class="ToolCodeBlock-module__container__EQXTk"><div class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeContainer__YJrZ9"><pre class="ToolCodeBlock-module__code__X8Whj"><code class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeWrap__HyFh6">$ sed -n <span class="hljs-string">'1,110p'</span> /home/runner/work/nn.nn/nn.nn/lang/a9nn/Personality.lua
-- a9nn/Personality.lua
-- Personality tensor system <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> cognitive agents.
--
-- A Personality is a fixed-size vector of trait dimensions, each <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> [0,1].
-- Traits can be <span class="hljs-built_in">read</span>/written individually and drift over <span class="hljs-keyword">time</span> <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> response to
-- experiences.  The system mirrors the <span class="hljs-string">"personality tensor"</span> concept described
-- <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> the NNECCO / Neuro-Sama architecture.

<span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> nn     = require(<span class="hljs-string">'a9nn.nn_ns'</span>)
<span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> Tensor = nn.Tensor
<span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> Class  = nn.Class

-- ─── Default trait <span class="hljs-built_in">set</span> ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

<span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> DEFAULT_TRAITS = {
   -- Cognitive
   <span class="hljs-string">"intelligence"</span>,   -- general reasoning ability
   <span class="hljs-string">"creativity"</span>,     -- divergent thinking
   <span class="hljs-string">"curiosity"</span>,      -- drive to explore
   <span class="hljs-string">"focus"</span>,          -- sustained attention

   -- Social / affective
   <span class="hljs-string">"empathy"</span>,        -- sensitivity to others
   <span class="hljs-string">"warmth"</span>,         -- friendliness
   <span class="hljs-string">"playfulness"</span>,    -- humour / levity
   <span class="hljs-string">"assertiveness"</span>,  -- confidence <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> expression

   -- Stability
   <span class="hljs-string">"stability"</span>,      -- emotional regulation
   <span class="hljs-string">"openness"</span>,       -- receptivity to new ideas
   <span class="hljs-string">"conscientiousness"</span>, -- reliability / thoroughness
   <span class="hljs-string">"resilience"</span>,     -- recovery from setbacks
}

-- ─── Personality ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

<span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> Personality, _ = Class.class(<span class="hljs-string">'nn.Personality'</span>)

--- @param traits  table of trait names (optional, uses DEFAULT_TRAITS)
--- @param values  table of initial values indexed by name or position (optional)
<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> Personality:__init(traits, values)
   self.traits     = traits or DEFAULT_TRAITS
   self.n          = <span class="hljs-comment">#self.traits</span>
   self.traitIndex = {}
   <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> i, name <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> ipairs(self.traits) <span class="hljs-keyword">do</span>
      self.traitIndex[name] = i
   end

   -- Underlying tensor (all 0.5 by default = neutral)
   self.tensor = Tensor.zeros(self.n)
   <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> i = 1, self.n <span class="hljs-keyword">do</span> self.tensor.data[i] = 0.5 end

   -- Apply supplied initial values
   <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> values <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span>
      <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> <span class="hljs-built_in">type</span>(values) == <span class="hljs-string">"table"</span> <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span>
         <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> k, v <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> pairs(values) <span class="hljs-keyword">do</span>
            <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> <span class="hljs-built_in">type</span>(k) == <span class="hljs-string">"string"</span> <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span>
               self:<span class="hljs-built_in">set</span>(k, v)
            elseif <span class="hljs-built_in">type</span>(k) == <span class="hljs-string">"number"</span> <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span>
               self.tensor.data[k] = math.min(1, math.max(0, v))
            end
         end
      end
   end

   -- Momentum buffer <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> drift updates
   self._momentum = Tensor.zeros(self.n)
   self.driftRate = 0.01
   self.momentumDecay = 0.9
end

--- Get the value of a single trait by name or index.
<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> Personality:get(nameOrIdx)
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> idx = <span class="hljs-built_in">type</span>(nameOrIdx) == <span class="hljs-string">"string"</span>
      and self.traitIndex[nameOrIdx] or nameOrIdx
   assert(idx, <span class="hljs-string">"Personality: unknown trait: "</span> .. tostring(nameOrIdx))
   <span class="hljs-built_in">return</span> self.tensor.data[idx]
end

--- Set a trait value (clamped to [0,1]).
<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> Personality:<span class="hljs-built_in">set</span>(nameOrIdx, value)
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> idx = <span class="hljs-built_in">type</span>(nameOrIdx) == <span class="hljs-string">"string"</span>
      and self.traitIndex[nameOrIdx] or nameOrIdx
   assert(idx, <span class="hljs-string">"Personality: unknown trait: "</span> .. tostring(nameOrIdx))
   self.tensor.data[idx] = math.min(1, math.max(0, value))
end

--- Apply an experience vector (delta per trait) with momentum dampening.
-- @param delta  Tensor or table of per-trait nudges (can be negative)
-- @param scale  overall learning rate <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> this experience (default 1.0)
<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> Personality:experience(delta, scale)
   scale = scale or 1.0
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> d = <span class="hljs-built_in">type</span>(delta) == <span class="hljs-string">"table"</span>
      and Tensor.new(delta) or delta

   -- Update momentum
   <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> i = 1, self.n <span class="hljs-keyword">do</span>
      self._momentum.data[i] = self.momentumDecay * self._momentum.data[i]
         + (1-self.momentumDecay) * d.data[i]
   end

   -- Apply drift
   <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> i = 1, self.n <span class="hljs-keyword">do</span>
      <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> newVal = self.tensor.data[i]
         + self.driftRate * scale * self._momentum.data[i]
      self.tensor.data[i] = math.min(1, math.max(0, newVal))
   end
end

--- Blend two personalities.
&lt;shellId: 5 completed with <span class="hljs-built_in">exit</span> code 0&gt;
</code></pre></div></figure></div></div></div></div><div class="Tool-module__toolContainer__PQCpK Tool-module__toolContainerExpanded__tkI3R" style="--tool-preview-height-threshold: 475px;"><div 9.7c-.2.2-.4.3-.6.3Z"></path></svg></span><span class="NewToolHeader-module__text__mpVp6"><span class="NewToolHeader-module__title__voLMB">Read EpisodicMemory core</span></span></button></div><div><div class="Tool-module__toolContentMissionControl__fqP1_"><div class="markdown-body MarkdownRenderer-module__container__sJ8jf" data-copilot-markdown="true" style="--MarkdownRenderer_streaming-fade-duration: 750ms;"><figure class="ToolCodeBlock-module__container__EQXTk"><div class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeContainer__YJrZ9"><pre class="ToolCodeBlock-module__code__X8Whj"><code class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeWrap__HyFh6">$ sed -n <span class="hljs-string">'30,120p'</span> /home/runner/work/nn.nn/nn.nn/lang/a9nn/EpisodicMemory.lua
-- @param experience  table with fields: state, action, reward, next_state, <span class="hljs-keyword">done</span>
-- @param priority    optional initial priority (defaults to max seen so far)
<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> EpisodicMemory:push(experience, priority)
   priority = priority or self.maxPriority
   self.buffer[self.head]     = experience
   self.priorities[self.head] = priority
   self.maxPriority = math.max(self.maxPriority, priority)
   self.head  = (self.head % self.capacity) + 1
   self.count = math.min(self.count + 1, self.capacity)
end

--- Sample a batch of `n` experiences.
-- @param n    batch size
-- @<span class="hljs-built_in">return</span>     table of experience tables, table of indices, table of IS-weights
<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> EpisodicMemory:sample(n)
   n = math.min(n, self.count)
   assert(n &gt; 0, <span class="hljs-string">"EpisodicMemory: empty buffer"</span>)

   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> experiences = {}
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> indices     = {}
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> weights     = {}

   <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> self.prioritised <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span>
      -- Compute probability distribution
      <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> prioSum = 0
      <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> i = 1, self.count <span class="hljs-keyword">do</span>
         prioSum = prioSum + (self.priorities[i]^self.alpha)
      end

      <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> minProb = (self.eps^self.alpha) / prioSum
      <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> maxW    = (minProb * self.count)^(-self.beta)

      -- Sample without replacement using a simple alias-free approach
      <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> chosen = {}
      <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> chosenSet = {}
      <span class="hljs-keyword">while</span> <span class="hljs-comment">#chosen &lt; n do</span>
         <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> r    = math.random() * prioSum
         <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> cum  = 0
         <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> added = <span class="hljs-literal">false</span>
         <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> i = 1, self.count <span class="hljs-keyword">do</span>
            cum = cum + (self.priorities[i]^self.alpha)
            <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> cum &gt;= r and not chosenSet[i] <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span>
               chosen[#chosen+1] = i
               chosenSet[i]      = <span class="hljs-literal">true</span>
               added = <span class="hljs-literal">true</span>
               <span class="hljs-built_in">break</span>
            end
         end
         -- safety: <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> weighted-random walk found no unchosen item
         -- (can happen when all items are already chosen), pick a random one
         <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> not added <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span>
            <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> _ = 1, self.count <span class="hljs-keyword">do</span>
               <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> idx = math.random(self.count)
               <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> not chosenSet[idx] <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span>
                  chosen[#chosen+1] = idx
                  chosenSet[idx]    = <span class="hljs-literal">true</span>
                  added = <span class="hljs-literal">true</span>
                  <span class="hljs-built_in">break</span>
               end
            end
            -- If still not added (all items exhausted), <span class="hljs-built_in">break</span> to avoid infinite loop
            <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> not added <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span> <span class="hljs-built_in">break</span> end
         end
      end

      <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> k, idx <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> ipairs(chosen) <span class="hljs-keyword">do</span>
         <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> prob = (self.priorities[idx]^self.alpha) / prioSum
         <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> w    = ((prob * self.count)^(-self.beta)) / maxW
         experiences[k] = self.buffer[idx]
         indices[k]     = idx
         weights[k]     = w
      end
   <span class="hljs-keyword">else</span>
      -- Uniform random sampling
      <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> pool = {}
      <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> i = 1, self.count <span class="hljs-keyword">do</span> pool[i] = i end
      -- Fisher-Yates shuffle first n
      <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> i = 1, n <span class="hljs-keyword">do</span>
         <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> j = math.random(i, <span class="hljs-comment">#pool)</span>
         pool[i], pool[j] = pool[j], pool[i]
      end
      <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> k = 1, n <span class="hljs-keyword">do</span>
         experiences[k] = self.buffer[pool[k]]
         indices[k]     = pool[k]
         weights[k]     = 1.0
      end
   end

   <span class="hljs-built_in">return</span> experiences, indices, weights
end

style="--MarkdownRenderer_streaming-fade-duration: 750ms;"><figure class="ToolCodeBlock-module__container__EQXTk"><div class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeContainer__YJrZ9"><pre class="ToolCodeBlock-module__code__X8Whj"><code class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeWrap__HyFh6">$ sed -n <span class="hljs-string">'1,120p'</span> /home/runner/work/nn.nn/nn.nn/lang/a9nn/LLaMAOrchestrator.lua
-- a9nn/LLaMAOrchestrator.lua
-- Orchestrator <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> 1-9 parallel LLaMA.cpp local-inference instances.
--
-- Each instance is assumed to be a running llama.cpp server (--server mode)
-- listening on a different HTTP port.  Requests are dispatched via the
-- `/completion` REST endpoint.
--
-- In environments without llama.cpp, the orchestrator falls back to a
-- stub <span class="hljs-string">"echo"</span> backend so that the cognitive pipeline can still run.
--
-- Port assignment: instance k uses port (basePort + k - 1).
-- Default base port: 8080.  Max instances: 9.

<span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> nn    = require(<span class="hljs-string">'a9nn.nn_ns'</span>)
<span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> Class = nn.Class

-- Try to load a lightweight HTTP library (<span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> present <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> the environment)
<span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> http = nil
pcall(<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span>()
   http = require(<span class="hljs-string">'socket.http'</span>)  -- LuaSocket
end)
<span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> not http <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span>
   pcall(<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span>()
      http = require(<span class="hljs-string">'http'</span>)       -- custom binding
   end)
end

-- ─── Instance record ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

<span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> <span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> makeInstance(<span class="hljs-built_in">id</span>, port)
   <span class="hljs-built_in">return</span> {
      <span class="hljs-built_in">id</span>           = <span class="hljs-built_in">id</span>,
      port         = port,
      available    = <span class="hljs-literal">true</span>,
      tokensServed = 0,
      requests     = 0,
      errors       = 0,
      lastLatencyMs = 0,
   }
end

-- ─── LLaMAOrchestrator ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

<span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> LLaMAOrchestrator, _ = Class.class(<span class="hljs-string">'nn.LLaMAOrchestrator'</span>)

--- Constructor.
-- @param opts   table:
--   numInstances  number of instances (1-9, default 1)
--   basePort      port <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> first instance (default 8080)
--   modelPath     path to .gguf model (informational)
--   <span class="hljs-built_in">timeout</span>       HTTP <span class="hljs-built_in">timeout</span> <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> seconds (default 30)
--   stubMode      <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> <span class="hljs-literal">true</span>, use <span class="hljs-built_in">echo</span> stub without real HTTP (default: auto)
<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> LLaMAOrchestrator:__init(opts)
   opts = opts or {}
   self.numInstances = math.min(9, math.max(1, opts.numInstances or 1))
   self.basePort     = opts.basePort  or 8080
   self.modelPath    = opts.modelPath or <span class="hljs-string">""</span>
   self.timeout      = opts.timeout   or 30
   self.stubMode     = opts.stubMode

   -- Auto-detect stub mode <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> no HTTP library available
   <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> self.stubMode == nil <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span>
      self.stubMode = (http == nil)
   end

   -- Build instance pool
   self.instances = {}
   <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> i = 1, self.numInstances <span class="hljs-keyword">do</span>
      self.instances[i] = makeInstance(i, self.basePort + i - 1)
   end

   -- Request queue
   self.queue = {}

   self.initialized = <span class="hljs-literal">false</span>
   self.verbose     = opts.verbose or <span class="hljs-literal">false</span>
end

--- Initialize (ping each instance to verify reachability).
-- In stub mode this always succeeds.
-- @<span class="hljs-built_in">return</span>  <span class="hljs-literal">true</span> <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> at least one instance is reachable
<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> LLaMAOrchestrator:initialize()
   <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> self.stubMode <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span>
      <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> self.verbose <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span>
         <span class="hljs-built_in">print</span>(string.format(
            <span class="hljs-string">"[LLaMAOrchestrator] Stub mode: %d virtual instance(s) on ports %d-%d"</span>,
            self.numInstances, self.basePort, self.basePort + self.numInstances - 1))
      end
      self.initialized = <span class="hljs-literal">true</span>
      <span class="hljs-built_in">return</span> <span class="hljs-literal">true</span>
   end

   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> ok = <span class="hljs-literal">false</span>
   <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> _, inst <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> ipairs(self.instances) <span class="hljs-keyword">do</span>
      <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> alive = self:_ping(inst)
      inst.available = alive
      <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> alive <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span> ok = <span class="hljs-literal">true</span> end
      <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> self.verbose <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span>
         <span class="hljs-built_in">print</span>(string.format(<span class="hljs-string">"[LLaMAOrchestrator] Instance %d (port %d): %s"</span>,
            inst.id, inst.port, alive and <span class="hljs-string">"UP"</span> or <span class="hljs-string">"DOWN"</span>))
      end
   end
   self.initialized = ok
   <span class="hljs-built_in">return</span> ok
end

--- Send a completion request.
-- @param prompt   string
-- @param params   table: temperature, max_tokens, top_p, stop
-- @<span class="hljs-built_in">return</span>         table: text, tokens, instance_id, latency_ms
<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> LLaMAOrchestrator:generate(prompt, params)
   params = params or {}
   assert(self.initialized, <span class="hljs-string">"LLaMAOrchestrator: call initialize() first"</span>)

   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> inst = self:_selectInstance()
   <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> not inst <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span>
      <span class="hljs-built_in">return</span> {text = <span class="hljs-string">""</span>, error = <span class="hljs-string">"No available instances"</span>}
   end

   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> t0 = os.clock()
&lt;shellId: 7 completed with <span class="hljs-built_in">exit</span> code 0&gt;
</code></pre></div></figure></div></div></div></div><div class="Tool-module__toolContainer__PQCpK Tool-module__toolContainerExpanded__tkI3R" style="--tool-preview-height-threshold: 475px;"><div data-copilot-markdown="true" style="--MarkdownRenderer_streaming-fade-duration: 750ms;"><h2>Phase 2 — <code node="[object Object]">lang/pli/a9nn.pli</code>: NNECCO Cognitive Agent in P-Systems</h2>
<p>Port <code node="[object Object]">a9nn/NNECCOAgent.lua</code> (+ EchoReservoir, Personality, EpisodicMemory, LLaMAOrchestrator) into native membrane computing, building on v2.3 <code node="[object Object]">atomspace.pli</code>.</p>
<ul class="contains-task-list">
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox" checked=""> Phase 1 <code node="[object Object]">atomspace.pli</code> merged (38 tests, 18 demos)</li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox"> Write <code node="[object Object]">lang/pli/a9nn.pli</code>:
<ul>
<li node="[object Object]">Echo State Reservoir membrane (leaky-integrator neurons, spectral radius, tanh)</li>
<li node="[object Object]">12-step <strong>EchoBeats</strong> loop as sequential rule phases (PERCEIVE…INTEGRATE) driven by a program-counter register</li>
<li node="[object Object]">Emotion Processing Unit (8 channels, novelty/reward-driven update)</li>
<li node="[object Object]">Consciousness layers L0–L3 with loss-driven meta-cognitive transitions</li>
<li node="[object Object]">PLAN beat: emotion-modulated argmax action selection</li>
<li node="[object Object]">Episodic memory membrane (priority-weighted experience tuples)</li>
<li node="[object Object]">LLaMA pool: 1–9 instance membranes with least-load antiport dispatch (stub mode)</li>
<li node="[object Object]">Hardware-style virtual registers (R0–R4, PC, STA)</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox"> Append a9nn tests to <code node="[object Object]">test_extensions.pli</code></li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox"> Append a9nn demo(s) to <code node="[object Object]">demo.pli</code></li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox"> Register <code node="[object Object]">a9nn</code> in <code node="[object Object]">integration.pli</code> module_registry</li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox"> Update <code node="[object Object]">lang/pli/README.md</code> (a9nn section + counts)</li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox"> <code node="[object Object]">bash lang/pli/validate.sh</code> passes</li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox"> Secret scan + CodeQL + commit/push + memory</li>
</ul>
<p>Numerical convention: integers ×100. No change to the shared XOR fixture (agent adds no float forward/loss semantics), so cross-port consistency is preserved.</p></div><div data-component="Stack" class="prc-Stack-Stack-UQ9k6" data-gap="condensed" data-direction="vertical" data-align="stretch" data-wrap="nowrap" data-justify="start" data-padding="normal"><span class="fgColor-muted prc-Text-Text-9mHv3" data-component="Text" data-size="small" data-weight="medium">Output</span><div class="markdown-body MarkdownRenderer-module__container__sJ8jf border rounded bgColor-default overflow-hidden" data-copilot-markdown="true" style="--MarkdownRenderer_streaming-fade-duration: 750ms;"><figure class="ToolCodeBlock-module__container__EQXTk"><div class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeContainer__YJrZ9"><pre class="ToolCodeBlock-module__code__X8Whj"><code class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeWrap__HyFh6">No changes to commit.   - Emotion Processing Unit (<span class="hljs-number">8</span> channels<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> novelty/reward-driven update)
  - Consciousness layers L0–L3 with loss-driven meta-cognitive transitions
  - PLAN beat<span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> emotion-modulated argmax action selection
  - Episodic memory membrane (priority-weighted experience tuples)
  - LLaMA pool<span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-number">1</span>–<span class="hljs-number">9</span> instance membranes with least-load antiport dispatch (stub mode)
  - Hardware-style virtual registers (R0–R4<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> PC<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> STA)
- <span class="hljs-punctuation">[</span> <span class="hljs-punctuation">]</span> Append a9nn tests to `test_extensions.pli`
- <span class="hljs-punctuation">[</span> <span class="hljs-punctuation">]</span> Append a9nn demo(s) to `demo.pli`
- <span class="hljs-punctuation">[</span> <span class="hljs-punctuation">]</span> Register `a9nn` in `integration.pli` module_registry

- <span class="hljs-punctuation">[</span> <span class="hljs-punctuation">]</span> `bash lang/pli/validate.sh` passes

<p>Port <code node="[object Object]">a9nn/NNECCOAgent.lua</code> (+ EchoReservoir, Personality, EpisodicMemory, LLaMAOrchestrator) into native membrane computing, building on v2.3 <code node="[object Object]">atomspace.pli</code>.</p>

<h2>What was built</h2>
<p><strong><code node="[object Object]">lang/pli/a9nn.pli</code></strong> (new, ~380 lines) — a faithful membrane-computing port of <code node="[object Object]">a9nn/NNECCOAgent.lua</code> and its cognitive stack, layered on the v2.3 <code node="[object Object]">atomspace.pli</code>:</p>
<table><thead><tr><th>NNECCO subsystem (Lua)</th><th>P-Systems mapping</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><strong>EchoReservoir</strong></td><td><code node="[object Object]">echo_reservoir</code> — membrane of leaky-integrator neurons, recurrent <code node="[object Object]">synapse{i,j,w}</code> objects, <code node="[object Object]">x'=(1-a)x + a·tanh(pre)</code>; one maximally-parallel step = one reservoir tick</td></tr><tr><td><strong>EchoBeats (12-step)</strong></td><td><code node="[object Object]">echobeats_driver</code> — a <strong>PC register (1..12)</strong> gates which rule phase fires, cycling PERCEIVE→…→INTEGRATE; the sequential method chain becomes a phase-locked parallel rule system</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Emotion Processing Unit</strong></td><td><code node="[object Object]">emotion_unit</code> — 8 channels (curiosity/joy/surprise/…) with the exact novelty/reward update laws from <code node="[object Object]">_beat_EMOTE</code></td></tr><tr><td><strong>Consciousness L0–L3</strong></td><td><code node="[object Object]">consciousness</code> — layer register moved by loss-driven REFLECT rules (<code node="[object Object]">&gt;0.5</code>→deliberate, <code node="[object Object]">&lt;0.01</code>→reactive)</td></tr><tr><td><strong>EpisodicMemory</strong></td><td><code node="[object Object]">episodic_memory</code> — circular membrane of experience tuples + priority objects, head wraps mod capacity</td></tr><tr><td><strong>LLaMAOrchestrator</strong></td><td><code node="[object Object]">llama_pool</code> — 1–9 instance membranes, <strong>least-load dispatch via antiport</strong>, stub-mode echo, ports <code node="[object Object]">base+k-1</code></td></tr><tr><td><strong>PLAN / policy</strong></td><td><code node="[object Object]">planner</code> — emotion-modulated argmax action selection</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Hardware registers</strong></td><td><code node="[object Object]">nnecco_agent</code> model — R0–R4, PC, STA as membrane objects; INTEGRATE writes an <code node="[object Object]">EpisodeNode</code> into the AtomSpace each cycle</td></tr></tbody></table>
<p><strong>Supporting changes:</strong></p>
<ul>
<li node="[object Object]"><code node="[object Object]">test_extensions.pli</code>: +8 tests → <strong>46 total</strong> (reservoir tick, PC cycling, emotion update, REFLECT, memory wrap, LLaMA least-load, PLAN argmax, INTEGRATE episode strength)</li>
<li node="[object Object]"><code node="[object Object]">demo.pli</code>: +1 NNECCO EchoBeats demo → <strong>19 total</strong></li>
<li node="[object Object]"><code node="[object Object]">integration.pli</code>: registry <code node="[object Object]">module{a9nn, "1.0.0", [nn, atomspace, integration]}</code></li>
<li node="[object Object]"><code node="[object Object]">README.md</code>: feature bullet, full a9nn API section, roadmap checkbox, v2.3 table row, counts (46 tests / 19 demos), coverage list</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Validation:</strong> all 25 <code node="[object Object]">.pli</code> files pass <code node="[object Object]">validate.sh</code>; secret scan clean; CodeQL N/A (PLingua/Markdown not analyzable). No change to the shared XOR fixture, so all 7 ports remain numerically consistent.</p>
<hr>
<h3>The full hybrid stack now exists in membrane computing 🎉</h3>
<p><code node="[object Object]">nn.pli</code> (deep learning) → <code node="[object Object]">gnn/attention/transformer</code> (architectures) → <code node="[object Object]">atomspace.pli</code> (symbolic hypergraph) → <code node="[object Object]">a9nn.pli</code> (embodied cognitive agent on top).</p>
<p><strong>Remaining frontier:</strong> the <strong>P-Limbo</strong> idea — a P-Systems variant targeting the Inferno/Dis distributed VM (membranes as Dis processes, communication rules as channel message-passing over Styx/9P, membrane tree = process tree). That's a genuinely different runtime, so I'd scope it as a <strong>design doc + minimal prototype</strong> in a new <code node="[object Object]">lang/limbo/</code> rather than PLingua. Want me to draft that next?</p></div></div><div class="SessionFooter-module__footer__uEbNi SessionFooter-module__metadataBar__VBpWD"><div class="SessionFooter-module__actions__unnyo"><div
